([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/bonferroni-multiple-testing-correction--c5141cf3432496d0))
- **bonferroni-multiple-testing-correction**: Use when many p-values are produced simultaneously (e.g., 48 K-S tests per lane pair on genome-partitioned read distributions) and per-combination significance calls must be corrected for multiple testing. - **bonferroni-multiple-testing-correction**: Use when many correlated biomarkers (e.g., 226 Nightingale NMR metabolomics measures) are simultaneously tested and an explicit family-wise alpha split (0.05 divided by the number of tests) is needed to declare significance or prune a model. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/bonferroni-multiple-testing-correction--e1ea591b960ee42e))
- **bonferroni-multiple-testing-correction**: Use when performing self-contained globaltest feature-set testing over spectral-similarity-derived k-medoids clusters in LC-MS/MS untargeted metabolomics data, and cluster-wise corrected P-values across multiple contrasts are required. - **bonferroni-multiple-testing-correction**: Use when a benchmarking workflow produces one P value per metabolite per feature set (e.g. Pearson correlations from 2,025 cross-validated RF models) and a per-metabolite significance call is needed. - **bonferroni-multiple-testing-correction**: Use when a univariate linear model is fitted on each column of an NMR peak-value feature matrix (e.g., 31 x 677 from speaq 2.0) and raw p-values must be family-wise corrected before declaring significant features. - **bonferroni-multiple-testing-correction**: Use when comparing abundances of many predicted functional modules (e.g. KEGG modules M00009, M00240) between sample groups and need family-wise error control over the resulting battery of statistical tests. - **bonferroni-multiple-testing-correction**: Use when a large family of statistical tests (e.g., 1641 motif enrichment Wilcoxon tests over a gene set) must be jointly thresholded, and raw p-values would otherwise yield inflated false positives. - **bonferroni-multiple-testing-correction**: Use when a per-gene model (e.g., the negative binomial GLM with Time, Group, and Time-by-Group interaction) yields thousands of raw p-values that must be Bonferroni-adjusted before calling tDEGs at p.cutoff = 0.05. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/bonferroni-multiple-testing-correction--b9ad0fbc27d7b21c))
- **bonferroni-multiple-testing-correction**: Use when many module-level or gene-set-level statistics (e.g., dozens of leiden-derived GRN module scores) are tested against the same phenotype and individual uncorrected p-values would inflate the false positive rate.
